- **diagnose-before-fix**: 出错后第一反应是回侦察与测量定位机制，不是改代码也不是逐字重跑。修复必须对应一个写下的根因。 ([HuangPuStar/MetaInfer](https://github.com/HuangPuStar/MetaInfer/tree/HEAD/atomic-skills/diagnose-before-fix) · ★139)

- **diagnose-before-retry**: When an operation fails, form a hypothesis from the logs/evidence before retrying — never retry blindly. Distinguish op-caused failures from self-caused artifacts (your own stops/kills), state the hypothesis with supporting context and appropriate uncertainty, and retry in the foreground with a timeout and observability so the retry is also a test of the hypothesis. Use when a long command (migrate, build, install, deploy, fetch) fails and you're tempted to just re-run it. ([jcdavis131/cursor-agent-skills](https://github.com/jcdavis131/cursor-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/diagnose-before-retry))

- **diagnosing-bugs**: Use when the user says diagnose or debug this, or reports something broken, throwing, failing, or slow. ([KunanonJ/superstacks](https://github.com/KunanonJ/superstacks/tree/HEAD/plugins/superstacks/skills/diagnosing-bugs) · ★5)
- **diagnosing-bugs**: Diagnose difficult or uncertain bugs and performance regressions with a reproducible feedback loop. Use when investigation is needed beyond an evident local fix. ([transitive-bullshit/skills](https://github.com/transitive-bullshit/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/diagnosing-bugs) · ★5)
- **diagnosing-bugs**: Diagnose hard bugs, flakes, regressions, and performance issues through reproduction, minimization, hypothesis, instrumentation, fix, and regression testing. Use when root cause is unclear, behavior is intermittent, a first fix failed, or the user asks to debug/diagnose a problem rather than simply implement known behavior. ([AnonymXXX/agent-harness-engineering](https://github.com/AnonymXXX/agent-harness-engineering/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/diagnosing-bugs) · ★4)
- **diagnosing-bugs**: Use when something is broken, throwing, failing, flaky, or slow and the cause is not obvious — "diagnose this", "debug this", "why is this failing?", "this is intermittent", "this got slow". Builds a feedback loop that goes red on the bug before theorising, then fixes with a regression test. Not for bugs whose cause you already know, and not for writing new features. ([baleen37/bstack](https://github.com/baleen37/bstack/tree/HEAD/plugins/me/skills/diagnosing-bugs) · ★4)
- **diagnosing-bugs**: Use when facing a bug with no obvious cause, a performance regression, or 5+ test failures at once. Triggers on: "diagnose", "debug this", "something broken", "no idea why", "flaky", "slow since", "fix multiple failing tests". ([dekoza/oh-my-slop](https://github.com/dekoza/oh-my-slop/tree/HEAD/skills/practice/diagnosing-bugs) · ★4)
- **diagnosing-bugs**: A disciplined loop for hard bugs, flaky tests and performance regressions - build a red-capable feedback loop first, reproduce and minimize, rank falsifiable hypotheses, instrument one variable at a time, then repair and remove the probes. Use when the user says "diagnose", "debug this" or "why is this failing", reports a wrong result, a crash, a timeout, a flaky test or a slowdown, or when a first-glance fix did not hold. ([ehnruiz/claude-skills](https://github.com/ehnruiz/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/diagnosing-bugs) · ★4)
- **diagnosing-bugs**: Phased loop for hard bugs - a failing or flaky test, a golden or CRC that moved, a worker-count or MSVC-vs-clang difference, a parity-harness mismatch, a crash, hang or desync, a slow tick or frame. Use when asked to diagnose, debug or find the cause of something broken, or before the second guess at a fix. Builds a fast red/green signal first, then ranked hypotheses, then the fix with a regression test. ([Gabriel-Barney/Empire-at-war-vulkan](https://github.com/Gabriel-Barney/Empire-at-war-vulkan/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/diagnosing-bugs) · ★4)
- **diagnosing-bugs**: Model-invoked discipline for debugging without guessing. Fire when something is broken and the cause is not obvious. Reproduce, minimize, hypothesize, instrument, then fix — and lock the fix with a regression test. Read the FIRST error in the chain, not the last. Stops the flailing-edit anti-pattern. Inspired by mattpocock/skills `/diagnosing-bugs`. ([ggichuru/cto-brain](https://github.com/ggichuru/cto-brain/tree/HEAD/skills/diagnosing-bugs) · ★4)
- **diagnosing-bugs**: Investigate an unclear failure, flake, or performance regression through falsifiable probes. Diagnosis alone does not authorize a fix. ([jimeh/agentic](https://github.com/jimeh/agentic/tree/HEAD/skills/diagnosing-bugs) · ★4)
- **diagnosing-bugs**: Bucle de diagnóstico para bugs difíciles y regresiones de rendimiento. Usar cuando el usuario diga "diagnostica"/"debuggea esto", o reporte algo roto, que lanza errores, que falla o que va lento. ([KroxiDev/engineering-skills](https://github.com/KroxiDev/engineering-skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/diagnosing-bugs) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **diagnosing-bugs**: Diagnosis loop for hard bugs and performance regressions. Use when asked to diagnose or debug, or when a hard bug needs a reproducible feedback loop. ([malinskibeniamin/skills](https://github.com/malinskibeniamin/skills/tree/HEAD/codex-skills/diagnosing-bugs) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **diagnosing-bugs**: Diagnosis loop for hard bugs and performance regressions. Use when asked to diagnose or debug something, or when something is reported broken, throwing, failing, stuck, wrong or slow. Builds a fast, red-capable feedback loop before any theory, then reproduces, minimises, tests ranked hypotheses, fixes with a regression test and cleans up, with a redaction rule for secrets and personal data. ([rknightion/synthkit](https://github.com/rknightion/synthkit/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/diagnosing-bugs) · ★4)
- **diagnosing-bugs**: Drives a reproduce, hypothesise, instrument, fix loop for hard bugs and performance regressions. Use when the user says 'diagnose' or 'debug this', or reports something broken, throwing, failing or slow. The `debugger` agent investigates one failure. ([rubakas/agent-notes](https://github.com/rubakas/agent-notes/tree/HEAD/.claude-plugin/skills/diagnosing-bugs) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **diagnosing-bugs**: 硬 bug 與效能回歸的診斷迴圈。當使用者說「diagnose」/「debug this」，或回報東西壞了/拋錯/失敗/很慢時使用。 ([shumingyang-opencode/mattpocock-skills-zh-tw](https://github.com/shumingyang-opencode/mattpocock-skills-zh-tw/tree/HEAD/skills/engineering/diagnosing-bugs) · ★4)
- **diagnosing-bugs**: 当遇到复杂 bug（并发/异步/竞态/非确定性复现/性能回退）或 anti-hallucination Step 5 加 log 3 轮未定位根因时使用。6 阶段：构建 tight feedback loop → 复现+最小化 → 假设 → 插桩 → 修复+回归测试 → 清理+复盘。关键词：复杂 bug、并发、竞态、性能回退、non-deterministic、feedback loop。 ([Stargazed-Dreamer/Parcae](https://github.com/Stargazed-Dreamer/Parcae/tree/HEAD/workspace/dev_toolkit/.agents/skills/diagnosing-bugs) · ★4)
- **diagnosing-bugs**: Diagnosis loop for hard bugs and performance regressions. Use when the user says diagnose/debug, or reports broken/failing/slow behavior. ([yugasun/aiops](https://github.com/yugasun/aiops/tree/HEAD/skills/diagnosing-bugs) · ★4)
- **diagnosing-bugs**: Diagnosis loop for hard bugs and performance regressions. Use when the user says "diagnose"/"debug this", or reports something broken/throwing/failing/slow. Only invoke via the orcinus router. ([DnsQueen/orcinus-skills](https://github.com/DnsQueen/orcinus-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/diagnosing-bugs) · ★3)
- **diagnosing-bugs**: 针对疑难 bug 和性能回归的诊断循环。当用户说"诊断"/"调试这个"，或报告某东西坏了/抛异常/失败/变慢时使用。 ([IsKenKenYa/skills](https://github.com/IsKenKenYa/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/engineering/diagnosing-bugs) · ★3)
- **diagnosing-bugs**: Use when the user says 'diagnose', 'debug this', reports something broken, throwing, failing, or slow, or when encountering a bug, test failure, or unexpected behavior and a fix is not yet obvious. ([Leostruka/devin-bundle](https://github.com/Leostruka/devin-bundle/tree/HEAD/skills/diagnosing-bugs) · ★3)
- **diagnosing-bugs**: 對付硬 bug、間歇性失敗與效能回歸的紀律迴圈 — 重現、最小化、立假說、下探針、修好、補回歸測試。當東西壞了而原因不明顯時使用，也對應 "flaky"、"regression"、「時好時壞」、「找不到原因」等說法。 ([Luca0x5755/luca-skills](https://github.com/Luca0x5755/luca-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/core/diagnosing-bugs) · ★3)
- **diagnosing-bugs**: Reproduction-first diagnosis loop for hard bugs and performance regressions. Use when the user says "diagnose"/"debug this", or reports something broken/throwing/failing/slow. ([luizomf/omskills](https://github.com/luizomf/omskills/tree/HEAD/skills/engineering/diagnosing-bugs) · ★3)
- **diagnosing-bugs**: 适用于根因不明确、反复出现、不稳定、跨组件或性能退化的故障。Use when a bug, test failure, runtime error, unexpected behavior, or performance regression has an unclear root cause, recurs, is nondeterministic, or spans components. 中文关键词：修 Bug、排查问题、诊断故障、根因不明确、反复出现、报错、测试失败、异常行为、性能回归。 ([madnpc/skills](https://github.com/madnpc/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/diagnosing-bugs) · ★3)
- **diagnosing-bugs**: Reproduce, isolate, and fix difficult bugs or performance regressions. Use when a bug, failure, or slowdown has no obvious cause, or when a first fix did not work. ([marcellocurto/skills](https://github.com/marcellocurto/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/diagnosing-bugs) · ★3)
- **diagnosing-bugs**: Discipline for diagnosing hard bugs and performance regressions — requires a reproduction loop that goes RED on the bug BEFORE any hypothesis (signal before theory). Finds the root cause and stops; the non-trivial fix is handed to spec-create. Use when the user asks to diagnose or debug something, or reports something broken, throwing, failing or slow. ([maurigre/mgr-method](https://github.com/maurigre/mgr-method/tree/HEAD/skills/diagnosing-bugs) · ★3)
- **diagnosing-bugs**: Diagnose bugs, test or build failures, unexpected behavior, and performance regressions with an evidence-first loop. Diagnosis alone does not authorize implementation. ([Sourav9063/ADD](https://github.com/Sourav9063/ADD/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/diagnosing-bugs) · ★3 · 3 copies)
- **diagnosing-bugs**: Diagnosis loop for hard bugs and performance regressions. Build a feedback loop that goes red on the bug before forming any theory, then minimise the repro, test 3–5 falsifiable hypotheses, instrument with tagged logs, fix the root cause behind a regression test, and clean up. Use when the user says "diagnose" or "debug this", or reports something broken, throwing, failing, flaky or slow, including a failing test or a broken build. - **diagnosing-bugs**: Diagnose difficult bugs and performance regressions through a symptom-specific reproduction, targeted experiments, and a proven fix. ([jwbaldwin/dotfiles](https://github.com/jwbaldwin/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/.config/opencode/skills/diagnosing-bugs))
- **diagnosing-bugs**: Runs a phased hard-bug workflow: invent a fast deterministic red-capable loop, minimise the repro, rank 3-5 falsifiable hypotheses, probe one variable, then land a seam-correct regression. Trigger on diagnose requests, stubborn flakes, throws, or performance regressions. Not a product-spec chair; never start hypothesizing before that loop has already been executed once. ([Kayforkind/skill-slice](https://github.com/Kayforkind/skill-slice/tree/HEAD/skills/diagnosing-bugs))
- **diagnosing-bugs**: Matt Pocock skill for hard bugs and performance regressions: build a tight feedback loop, reproduce, minimize, hypothesize, instrument, fix, regression-test. ([klaachkinggit/klaach_harness](https://github.com/klaachkinggit/klaach_harness/tree/HEAD/.codex/skills/diagnosing-bugs))
- **diagnosing-bugs**: Use when a claim is routed to this project's shipped-behaviour track — a defect (the running system misbehaves) or an enhancement (its current behaviour should change) — and has to be confirmed or refuted with a reproduction before anything is specified, ticketed or fixed. Not for a test that fails while implementing a planned task (the `development` work type owns that), and not for the fix itself. Interactive only — it leans on a human partner who has the running installation, so never self-invoke it in a non-interactive context. ([kristofdegrave/homeassistant-smart-charging](https://github.com/kristofdegrave/homeassistant-smart-charging/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/diagnosing-bugs))
- **diagnosing-bugs**: Diagnosis loop for hard bugs and performance regressions — refuses to theorise until a tight feedback loop already goes red on this bug. Use when the user says "diagnose" or "debug this", or reports something broken, throwing, failing, flaky, or slow. ([krukovden/skill-catalog](https://github.com/krukovden/skill-catalog/tree/HEAD/skills/engineering/diagnosing-bugs))
- **diagnosing-bugs**: Diagnosis loop for hard bugs and performance regressions. Use when the user says "diagnose" or "debug this", or reports something broken, throwing, failing or slow. ([kzarzycki/agent-skills](https://github.com/kzarzycki/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/diagnosing-bugs) · 5 copies)
- **diagnosing-bugs**: Diagnosis loop for hard bugs and performance regressions in .NET codebases. Use when the user says "diagnose"/"debug this", or reports something broken/throwing/failing/slow. ([lamuniercosta/dotnet-agent-harness](https://github.com/lamuniercosta/dotnet-agent-harness/tree/HEAD/skills/diagnosing-bugs) · 3 copies)
- **diagnosing-bugs**: 当用户说"帮我查这个 bug""诊断一下""为什么报错/变慢/偶发失败"，且问题不是一眼能看出来的时候使用。六阶段纪律：先造一个能变红的反馈回路，再复现并最小化，再列 3–5 个可证伪假设，再逐个插桩验证，再先写回归测试后修复，最后清理。与 systematic-debugging 的区别：本技能把"反馈回路"作为硬门禁，不建回路不许猜。 ([LeadingThink/miniQ](https://github.com/LeadingThink/miniQ/tree/HEAD/crates/miniq-plugins/bundled/miniq.official.dev-flow/diagnosing-bugs))
- **diagnosing-bugs**: 어려운 버그와 성능 회귀를 위한 진단 루프. 사용자가 'diagnose'/'debug this'라고 말하거나, 무언가가 깨졌다/던지고 있다/실패하고 있다/느리다고 보고할 때 사용하세요. ([lee-chance/ai-skills-ko](https://github.com/lee-chance/ai-skills-ko/tree/HEAD/matt-pocock-skills-ko/skills/engineering/diagnosing-bugs))

([drmikecrowe/harnessed](https://github.com/drmikecrowe/harnessed/tree/HEAD/catalog/recipes/mikes-universal-setup/skills/diagnosis-discipline))
- **diagnosis-evidence**: Make a diagnosis prove it measured what it claims — before reporting a cause, a "not a regression", a mutation check, or a passing verification. Use when debugging a failing test, a CI failure, a flake, or verifying a fix by hand. ([kamiazya/whiteboard](https://github.com/kamiazya/whiteboard/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/diagnosis-evidence) · ★11)
- **diagnosis-health**: 当需要诊断产品健康度时使用。产品健康度自动诊断，采集多维度数据并进行综合评分、趋势预测和瓶颈识别，输出健康度报告。关键词：健康度评分、产品诊断、多维度评分、健康检查、产品健康、健康评分、产品体检、状态好不好。 ([LuckyOneTwoThree/vibe-skill](https://github.com/LuckyOneTwoThree/vibe-skill/tree/HEAD/pm-skill/pm-07-monitoring/skills/diagnosis-health) · ★37 · 2 copies)
- **diagnosis-health**: Use when you need to diagnose product health. Automated product health diagnosis that collects multi-dimensional data and performs comprehensive scoring, trend prediction, and bottleneck identification, outputting a health report. Keywords: health score, product diagnosis, multi-dimensional scoring, health check, product health, health rating, product checkup, status check.
